# Fix rank 100058: fix(i18n): refresh inbound debounce help translations

- sha: `5807e3bbbe426789c66d7b23ba3b0c438fd485ff`
- date: 2026-09-22T14:26:34-07:00
- author: Peter Steinberger
- parent: `5807e3bbbe426789c66d7b23ba3b0c438fd485ff^`

## Fix message body

```text
The inbound debounce help changed on main after the previous locale
refresh. Its generated catalog key and source hash no longer matched
translation memory, blocking the strict release preflight.

Regenerate the twenty locale memories and metadata through the canonical
sync owner. Each locale replaces the obsolete help entry; strict catalog
validation reports zero fallbacks.
```

## Fix PR

(no PR number in subject, or fetch failed)

+{"cache_key":"2bbb97e59b06f30845f8ca8ecf86fb2b95464b8d804078a561c6d47d97b114ad","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/es.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"es","translated":"Intervalo de inactividad (ms) para agrupar mensajes entrantes enviados rápidamente por el mismo remitente. Telegram usa 300 ms de forma predeterminada cuando no se ha configurado un valor global ni uno específico del canal; los demás canales usan 0. El valor cero desactiva la agrupación habitual de ráfagas de mensajes, pero Telegram sigue uniendo los fragmentos de mensajes largos cercanos al límite.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:37:31.046Z"}

 {"cache_key":"6b4516dd1458ca934f55e639d254ba3668b516eca6b703ef1d8b6b212622349a","segment_id":"chat.workspaceConflict.commandHelp","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/fa.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Run these in Bash or zsh (Git Bash on Windows). If inspect says the path does not exist, the cloud deleted it; verify and remove the local path manually. If checkout reports a file/directory conflict, move or remove the blocking local path, then retry. If the staged ref is missing, the notice is stale; do not change the local path.","text_hash":"9230285138ce5b558be08a2c989463f6ca469a6bcdeaa199716a941e1b496a53","tgt_lang":"fa","translated":"این فرمان‌ها را در Bash یا zsh (در Windows با Git Bash) اجرا کنید. اگر فرمان بررسی اعلام کرد که مسیر وجود ندارد، در فضای ابری حذف شده است; پس از اطمینان، مسیر محلی را به‌صورت دستی حذف کنید. اگر checkout تداخل فایل/پوشه گزارش داد، مسیر محلیِ مانع را جابه‌جا یا حذف کنید، سپس دوباره تلاش کنید. اگر مرجع آماده‌شده موجود نیست، اعلان قدیمی است; مسیر محلی را تغییر ندهید.","updated_at":"2026-09-04T10:45:13.540Z"}

+{"cache_key":"6b4a64d7f7809094fe4ceab7d68726d70f00cecb4be10709b1c203056ee67bd8","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/fa.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"fa","translated":"بازهٔ انتظار بدون دریافت پیام (میلی‌ثانیه) برای تجمیع پیام‌های ورودی پیاپی از یک فرستنده. اگر مقدار جایگزینی در سطح سراسری یا کانال تنظیم نشده باشد، مقدار پیش‌فرض Telegram برابر با ۳۰۰ میلی‌ثانیه است؛ مقدار پیش‌فرض سایر کانال‌ها ۰ است. صفر، تجمیع معمول پیام‌های پیاپی را غیرفعال می‌کند، اما Telegram همچنان قطعه‌های پیام‌های بلندِ نزدیک به سقف طول مجاز را به هم متصل می‌کند.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:47.045Z"}

+{"cache_key":"ccb412a7af8c1a2e2b31daac83885d61877e597866e0259af52a608727702f13","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/fr.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"fr","translated":"Délai d’inactivité (ms) permettant de regrouper les messages entrants rapprochés d’un même expéditeur. Telegram utilise par défaut 300 ms lorsqu’aucune valeur de remplacement n’est définie globalement ou pour le canal ; les autres canaux utilisent 0 par défaut. Une valeur de zéro désactive le regroupement habituel des messages en rafale, mais Telegram réassemble toujours les fragments de messages longs dont la taille approche la limite.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:37:46.956Z"}
 {"cache_key":"ccb51b25017cae7f3c5ae26a2556e5bf3a53fb1bbfbc1aac49312a82fd183988","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EbyChannel.help.v1-wbz54j-4g","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/fr.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Per-channel inbound debounce overrides keyed by provider id in milliseconds. Use this where some providers send message fragments more aggressively than others.","text_hash":"926ba74d05e309e026bf2f1864f2f6f7e2a967bca967ba82531e5c43bcf403e5","tgt_lang":"fr","translated":"Délais de regroupement des messages entrants propres à chaque canal, en millisecondes, indexés par identifiant de fournisseur. Utilisez ce réglage lorsque certains fournisseurs envoient des fragments de messages à une cadence plus élevée que d’autres.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T17:31:28.993Z"}

+{"cache_key":"003a357ae27d5c0b971aedb5472ca6e3bcf839f7da748f076afbea85ad89ab7a","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/id.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"id","translated":"Jeda tunggu (ms) untuk mengelompokkan pesan masuk yang dikirim beruntun oleh pengirim yang sama. Telegram menggunakan nilai default 300ms jika tidak ada pengaturan pengganti global maupun per kanal; kanal lain menggunakan nilai default 0. Nilai nol menonaktifkan pengelompokan pesan beruntun biasa, tetapi Telegram tetap menggabungkan kembali potongan pesan panjang yang mendekati batas panjang pesan.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:18.574Z"}

-{"cache_key":"19b62cfe4c5077eb3f90007a3353a7520b19ab423a759d79e26e9cc7588c0d41","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1q219rf-2l","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/nl.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Debounce window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender (0 to disable).","text_hash":"13c9614fac9e249447cf6a6bf6889128bb641198d774f1299cf2d221c98284a2","tgt_lang":"nl","translated":"Debounce-tijdvenster (ms) om snel opeenvolgende inkomende berichten van dezelfde afzender te bundelen (0 om uit te schakelen).","updated_at":"2026-09-09T19:18:39.186Z"}
 {"cache_key":"19ba843e3ccfab2c254959ed9c72c17e28b39bdb805d2545ddf942646c6c4949","segment_id":"configHints.tools%2EagentToAgent.help.v1-1ynpa5f-cf","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/nl.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Policy for cross-agent session tool calls: sends, list, history, search, and status reads (default: enabled). Use allow to restrict agent pairs; enabled=false blocks ordinary cross-agent access. Requester-owned native subagent and ACP child sessions stay reachable under tree or all visibility. For strict separation, set tools.sessions.visibility to agent or self (tree still admits requester-owned native/ACP children), or use separate gateways.","text_hash":"cc2eec141105805e700ff98ea169c70ad3e67df6945a8baa6ce497ec47e72d96","tgt_lang":"nl","translated":"Beleid voor sessietoolaanroepen tussen agents: verzenden, weergeven van lijsten, geschiedenis, zoeken en uitlezen van status (standaard: ingeschakeld). Gebruik allow om agentparen te beperken; enabled=false blokkeert gewone toegang tussen agents. Native subagentsessies en ACP-onderliggende sessies die eigendom zijn van de aanvrager, blijven bereikbaar met zichtbaarheid tree of all. Stel voor strikte scheiding tools.sessions.visibility in op agent of self (tree laat nog steeds native/ACP-onderliggende sessies van de aanvrager toe), of gebruik afzonderlijke gateways.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T19:25:50.379Z"}

+{"cache_key":"3a4f7043d21d1aced444f9ff8f54de5845c0d7a8a8bb2a141ea0f2b07d76efb1","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/nl.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"nl","translated":"Wachttijd zonder nieuwe berichten (ms) om snel opeenvolgende inkomende berichten van dezelfde afzender te bundelen. Telegram gebruikt standaard 300 ms als er geen globale of kanaalspecifieke instelling is opgegeven; andere kanalen gebruiken standaard 0. Nul schakelt het bundelen van gewone berichtenreeksen uit, maar Telegram voegt fragmenten van lange berichten die de lengtelimiet naderen nog steeds samen.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:41.228Z"}

+{"cache_key":"af792d18f97cc130ab5294833faaf58c516941a330e52ec8d24ded696eb56faf","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/pt-BR.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"pt-BR","translated":"Janela de inatividade (ms) para agrupar mensagens recebidas em rápida sequência do mesmo remetente. O Telegram usa 300 ms por padrão quando não há uma substituição global nem por canal; os outros canais usam 0 por padrão. Zero desativa o agrupamento comum de mensagens em sequência, mas o Telegram ainda reagrupa fragmentos de mensagens longas próximas do limite.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:37:18.536Z"}

Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"ru","translated":"Период ожидания без новых сообщений (мс) для объединения быстро поступающих сообщений от одного отправителя.  {"cache_key":"af549cde283c658d32ffb9cb48074c855849585a2978af400593fb8361a41ce8","segment_id":"configHints.gateway%2Eauth%2EtrustedProxy%2EdeviceAutoApprove%2Escopes.help.v1-raeila-g3","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/tr.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Maximum scopes granted to auto-approved operator devices. Defaults to operator.read, operator.write, operator.approvals, and operator.questions. Requested scopes are capped to this list; requests without scopes receive this list. When unset, auto-approval also adds operator.questions for older UI clients; an explicit list is never widened. Explicitly listing operator.admin lets every proxy-authenticated user auto-approve full admin and makes scope-less requests receive full admin automatically; it also triggers a critical security audit finding and Gateway startup warning.","text_hash":"962919d68dc2d48492881130eb5b5c5eb17f7aa165a87db54059fee635562d96","tgt_lang":"tr","translated":"Otomatik onaylanan operatör cihazlarına verilen en geniş yetki kapsamları. Varsayılan değerler operator.read, operator.write, operator.approvals ve operator.questions şeklindedir. İstenen kapsamlar bu listeyle sınırlıdır; kapsam belirtilmeyen isteklere bu liste atanır. Ayarlanmadığında, otomatik onay eski UI istemcileri için operator.questions kapsamını da ekler; açıkça belirtilen bir liste hiçbir zaman genişletilmez. operator.admin kapsamını açıkça listelemek, proxy üzerinden kimliği doğrulanan her kullanıcının tam yönetici yetkisi için otomatik onay almasına ve kapsam belirtilmeyen isteklere otomatik olarak tam yönetici yetkisi verilmesine olanak tanır; ayrıca kritik bir güvenlik denetimi bulgusunu ve Gateway başlangıç uyarısını tetikler.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T17:59:55.264Z"}
 {"cache_key":"af588c78d43d4bb461e57a62eb2cff7fb537b8c80f76622417db8f9ee4a72451","segment_id":"configHints.models%2Eproviders%2E*%2Erequest%2Eproxy%2Etls%2Ecert.help.v1-x8nk2q-22","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/tr.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Client TLS certificate presented to the proxy when mutual TLS is required.","text_hash":"6c5f68463ff2693048c9e7192b0aa64dc34fb5858416bb6cd07963e870d2d94a","tgt_lang":"tr","translated":"Karşılıklı TLS gerektiğinde proxy'ye sunulan istemci TLS sertifikası.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T18:09:33.945Z"}
+{"cache_key":"af6522aaaa374ed2536dcae67620b670b04f9eb03d19627b0c87d51303113479","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/tr.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"tr","translated":"Aynı göndericiden art arda hızla gelen mesajları gruplamak için beklenecek sessizlik süresi (ms). Genel düzeyde veya kanal düzeyinde bir geçersiz kılma ayarı belirtilmediğinde Telegram varsayılan olarak 300 ms kullanır; diğer kanallarda varsayılan değer 0'dır. Sıfır, art arda gelen mesajların normal şekilde gruplanmasını devre dışı bırakır, ancak Telegram yine de uzun mesajların boyut sınırına yakın parçalarını yeniden birleştirir.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:09.250Z"}

 {"cache_key":"b3af4587227dded5b1e4232f85f9566b1bebfa83834e1902d0f1dba90569e639","segment_id":"chat.queue.full","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/uk.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"The message queue is full.  {"cache_key":"b3b787772815fd90cb2df074a487e7c4d4b3d8406ff1477eb91122ed2e61b729","segment_id":"configHints.auth%2Eprofiles.help.v1-kzoqcn-1j","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/uk.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Named auth profiles (provider + mode + optional email).","text_hash":"e35297c14216940012be413e2b2b9d161ef2dce6f1866ad369595fc76175e05c","tgt_lang":"uk","translated":"Іменовані профілі автентифікації (постачальник + режим + необов’язкова електронна адреса).","updated_at":"2026-09-09T18:24:31.340Z"}
+{"cache_key":"b3c08e5dc20148313a279791a7e216beeba0660f71065c1a1428fddc60f5eaee","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/uk.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"uk","translated":"Період очікування без нових повідомлень (мс) для об’єднання в пакет вхідних повідомлень, що швидко надходять від одного відправника. Для Telegram типове значення становить 300 мс, якщо не задано ні глобального перевизначення, ні перевизначення для каналу; для інших каналів — 0. Нуль вимикає звичайне пакетне об’єднання серій повідомлень, але Telegram усе одно об’єднує фрагменти довгих повідомлень, розмір яких наближається до граничного.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:14.160Z"}
 {"cache_key":"b3c44f14b50a06717238eff9e19950960501d52964d3ce419e66e476dae38b60","segment_id":"configHints.plugins%2Eenabled.help.v1-pnygd7-50","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/uk.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Enable or disable plugin/extension loading globally during startup and config reload (default: true). Залишайте ввімкненим, лише якщо у вашому розгортанні потрібні можливості розширень.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T18:45:54.773Z"}

 {"cache_key":"34c6f649f253a0c5e3b78d3a4d750233ae2103e8e978ce94b155a5733ca6c199","segment_id":"appsPage.cards.linux.desc","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/vi.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Native desktop app — .deb and AppImage builds.","text_hash":"dfac3e543f7625752a1306478a7b507056ff856f3c1f1935e1a7e43a52cafa01","tgt_lang":"vi","translated":"Ứng dụng gốc cho máy tính — các bản dựng .deb và AppImage.","updated_at":"2026-09-04T10:21:46.843Z"}
+{"cache_key":"34cb8c341db8885e12d873c0fbbb647123da3cbba39eefc59dfcfa3f881ceebe","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2Einbound%2EdebounceMs.help.v1-1616yo2-7w","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/vi.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Quiet window (ms) for batching rapid inbound messages from the same sender. Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"vi","translated":"Khoảng chờ yên lặng (ms) để gom các tin nhắn đến liên tiếp từ cùng một người gửi thành một đợt. Telegram mặc định dùng 300ms khi không có giá trị ghi đè ở cấp toàn cục hoặc kênh; các kênh khác mặc định dùng 0. Giá trị 0 tắt tính năng gom đợt tin nhắn thông thường, nhưng Telegram vẫn ghép lại các phần của tin nhắn dài có độ dài gần giới hạn.","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:38:35.514Z"}
 {"cache_key":"34e3e19ce0012ca0fe33fd0a69b56387a12df5560185389c7b94deb19bc540fd","segment_id":"configHints.messages%2EgroupChat%2EhistoryLimit.help.v1-1mkbpw-9k","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/vi.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Maximum number of prior group messages loaded as context per turn for group sessions. Use higher values for richer continuity, or lower values for faster and cheaper responses. Automatic prompt history is capped at 200 messages. The JSON integer maximum selects the channel default window (50 for shared group history), not an unlimited window.","text_hash":"94d17710cb032fb8008c603619d30657eeb19f78d5f4c3c810a21d7d44146a8e","tgt_lang":"vi","translated":"Số lượng tối đa tin nhắn nhóm trước đó được tải làm ngữ cảnh mỗi lượt trong các phiên nhóm. Dùng giá trị cao hơn để duy trì mạch hội thoại đầy đủ hơn, hoặc giá trị thấp hơn để phản hồi nhanh hơn và tiết kiệm chi phí hơn. Lịch sử tự động đưa vào lời nhắc được giới hạn ở 200 tin nhắn. Giá trị số nguyên tối đa của JSON sẽ chọn phạm vi lịch sử mặc định của kênh (50 tin nhắn đối với lịch sử nhóm dùng chung), chứ không phải phạm vi không giới hạn.","updated_at":"2026-09-21T23:47:08.287Z"}

 {"cache_key":"6481eb98bf0d4f83b8f95e62133da4831de2728613c09780ad31c5627aa2c5df","segment_id":"configHints.agents%2Edefaults%2Emodels%2E*%2EcodeMode.help.v1-rsdl5o-6x","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/vi.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"OpenClaw Code Mode for this exact provider/model: On forces it on, Off disables it, and Default inherits tools.codeMode.enabled. This does not change the selected runtime or Codex native Code Mode.","text_hash":"af0d7c79c67752a7712c888d2f9289fb62df5c480b92d5456c9f8eb3c47029fb","tgt_lang":"vi","translated":"Chế độ lập trình OpenClaw cho đúng nhà cung cấp/mô hình này: Bật buộc kích hoạt chế độ này, Tắt vô hiệu hóa chế độ này, còn Mặc định kế thừa tools.codeMode.enabled. Các thiết lập kích hoạt riêng của tác nhân được ưu tiên. Thiết lập này không thay đổi môi trường thực thi đã chọn hoặc chế độ lập trình gốc của Codex.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T18:51:44.653Z"}

Telegram defaults to 300ms when neither a global nor channel override is set; other channels default to 0. Zero disables ordinary burst batching, but Telegram still reassembles near-limit long-message chunks.","text_hash":"5768881fa4b3cdd03578b8621b38d8d1ebac68d23203d0ab6258a9f821485677","tgt_lang":"zh-CN","translated":"用于合并同一发送者快速连续发来的消息的静默窗口（毫秒）。未设置全局或渠道覆盖值时，Telegram 默认为 300 毫秒，其他渠道默认为 0。设为 0 会禁用常规的突发消息合并，但 Telegram 仍会重新拼接接近长度上限的长消息分片。","updated_at":"2026-09-22T20:37:10.062Z"}

 {"cache_key":"11a224f17d2d9c474d417730bb5a393b7efa9d92073e130ca41d76a3ec272675","segment_id":"configHints.logging%2Eaudit%2Emessages.help.v1-1yg4lc3-76","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/ar.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Controls content-free message lifecycle records: \"off\" (default), \"direct\" for known direct conversations only, or \"all\" for direct, group, channel, and unknown conversation kinds. +{"cache_key":"05335108186a3789e4378a421b30b38fb0f7b40a09b82d7a65e9da23978da84f","segment_id":"configHints.session%2Emaintenance%2EmaxDiskBytes.help.v1-147lxk3-cj","source_path":"ui/src/i18n/locales/ar.ts","src_lang":"en","text":"Per-agent physical disk budget covering SQLite main/WAL and counted session-directory artifacts (for example `500mb`). Defaults to `10gb`; protected data can keep usage above the target. When exceeded, warn mode reports pressure and enforce mode removes old reset/delete artifacts, unreferenced history, then cap-created archives. Manual, age-retention, dashboard, recovery, and legacy archives remain protected. Set `false`, `0`, or `\"0\"` to disable.","text_hash":"9e7fba99efdf3a1b8940c6cec665ebd84246805440326502272464fab3956d41","tgt_lang":"ar","translated":"ميزانية مساحة القرص الفعلية لكل وكيل، وتشمل ملف SQLite الرئيسي وملف WAL والعناصر المحتسبة في دليل الجلسات (مثل `500mb`). القيمة الافتراضية هي `10gb`؛ وقد تُبقي البيانات المحمية الاستخدام أعلى من الحد المستهدف. عند تجاوزه، يُبلغ وضع التحذير عن ضغط المساحة، بينما يزيل وضع الإنفاذ العناصر القديمة الناتجة عن إعادة التعيين أو الحذف، ثم السجل غير المشار إليه، ثم الأرشيفات المنشأة بسبب الحد الأقصى. تظل الأرشيفات اليدوية، وأرشيفات الاحتفاظ حسب العمر، ولوحة المعلومات، والاسترداد، والأرشيفات القديمة محمية. عيّن القيمة إلى `false` أو `0` أو `\"0\"` للتعطيل.","updated_at":"2026-09-09T18:06:50.013Z"}
